## CreateAccountingAccount

Creates a AccountingAccount. Provide the unified AccountingAccount fields in the request body; they are mapped to the provider's API. Returns the created AccountingAccount.

### Example Usage

<!-- UsageSnippet language="go" operationID="createAccountingAccount" method="post" path="/accounting/{connection_id}/account" example="accounting_account" -->
```go
package main

import(
	"context"
	unifiedgosdk "github.com/unified-to/unified-go-sdk"
	"github.com/unified-to/unified-go-sdk/pkg/types"
	"github.com/unified-to/unified-go-sdk/pkg/models/shared"
	"github.com/unified-to/unified-go-sdk/pkg/models/operations"
	"log"
)

func main() {
    ctx := context.Background()

    s := unifiedgosdk.New(
        unifiedgosdk.WithSecurity("<YOUR_API_KEY_HERE>"),
    )

    res, err := s.Accounting.CreateAccountingAccount(ctx, operations.CreateAccountingAccountRequest{
        AccountingAccount: shared.AccountingAccount{
            Balance: unifiedgosdk.Pointer[float64](12092.0),
            CreatedAt: types.MustNewTimeFromString("2022-07-03T17:57:07.391Z"),
            Currency: unifiedgosdk.Pointer("CAD"),
            CustomerDefinedCode: unifiedgosdk.Pointer("6170"),
            Description: unifiedgosdk.Pointer("Voluptatibus valde spoliatio."),
            ID: unifiedgosdk.Pointer("66281ff5-5a74-4c18-9e6d-1275fb4d66d2"),
            IsPayable: unifiedgosdk.Pointer(false),
            Metadata: []shared.AccountingMetadata{},
            Name: unifiedgosdk.Pointer("Auto Loan Account"),
            Status: shared.StatusArchived.ToPointer(),
            Taxonomy: []shared.AccountingAccountTaxonomy{
                shared.AccountingAccountTaxonomy{
                    OriginalType: unifiedgosdk.Pointer("vilitas"),
                    Type: shared.AccountingAccountTaxonomyTypeSubgroup,
                    Value: "comedo",
                },
            },
            Type: shared.TypeAccountsReceivable.ToPointer(),
            UpdatedAt: types.MustNewTimeFromString("2023-08-09T22:11:40.715Z"),
        },
        ConnectionID: "<id>",
    })
    if err != nil {
        log.Fatal(err)
    }
    if res.AccountingAccount != nil {
        // handle response
    }
}
```

| `ctx`                                                                                                      | [context.Context](https://pkg.go.dev/context#Context)                                                      | :heavy_check_mark:                                                                                         | The context to use for the request.                                                                        |

```go
	"context"
```

```go
    res, err := s.Accounting.CreateAccountingBill(ctx, operations.CreateAccountingBillRequest{
        AccountingBill: shared.AccountingBill{

            CategoryIds: []string{},

            Currency: unifiedgosdk.Pointer("USD"),

            Metadata: []shared.AccountingMetadata{},
```

```go
	"context"
	unifiedgosdk "github.com/unified-to/unified-go-sdk"
	"github.com/unified-to/unified-go-sdk/pkg/types"
	"github.com/unified-to/unified-go-sdk/pkg/models/shared"
	"github.com/unified-to/unified-go-sdk/pkg/models/operations"
	"log"
)

func main() {
    ctx := context.Background()

    s := unifiedgosdk.New(
        unifiedgosdk.WithSecurity("<YOUR_API_KEY_HERE>"),
    )

    res, err := s.Accounting.CreateAccountingTransaction(ctx, operations.CreateAccountingTransactionRequest{
        AccountingTransaction: shared.AccountingTransaction{
            CreatedAt: types.MustNewTimeFromString("2019-09-25T11:40:42.574Z"),
            ID: unifiedgosdk.Pointer("58980b6c-f5b1-421c-9bc1-b2bd02413c52"),
            Lineitems: []shared.AccountingTransactionLineItem{
                shared.AccountingTransactionLineItem{
                    CategoryIds: []string{},
                    Description: unifiedgosdk.Pointer("The turquoise Hat combines Wallis and Futuna aesthetics with Potassium-based durability"),
                    ID: unifiedgosdk.Pointer("31b55050-287f-4738-9bc4-2aa67a1784d9"),
                    Name: unifiedgosdk.Pointer("Shirt"),
                    TotalAmount: unifiedgosdk.Pointer[float64](10184.0),
                    UnitAmount: unifiedgosdk.Pointer[float64](536.0),
                    UnitQuantity: unifiedgosdk.Pointer[float64](19.0),
                },
            },
            Memo: unifiedgosdk.Pointer("deposit of ISK 792.81 at Fritsch - Dooley charged to account ending in 7879 using card ending in ****3230."),
            Status: shared.AccountingTransactionStatusPending.ToPointer(),
            TaxAmount: unifiedgosdk.Pointer[float64](0.0),
            TotalAmount: unifiedgosdk.Pointer[float64](10184.0),
            TransactionAt: types.MustNewTimeFromString("2019-09-25T11:40:42.574Z"),
            UpdatedAt: types.MustNewTimeFromString("2019-10-14T08:12:53.026Z"),
        },
        ConnectionID: "<id>",
    })
    if err != nil {
        log.Fatal(err)
    }
    if res.AccountingTransaction != nil {
        // handle response
    }
}
```
